# [Bugfix][Compilation] Write cache index atomically

[Upstream PR](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60440) · state: closed · merged: False

## Original PR description

## Overview

Write the compile cache index through a same-directory temporary file and atomically replace the old index. Closes #60410.

## Claims

- Concurrent readers now see either the previous complete cache or the next complete cache, never a partially written index.
- A failed write leaves the previous index in place.

## Validation

- `HF_HOME=/workspace/.setup/hf-home .venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/compile/test_compiler_cache.py -q` — 1 passed.
- `pre-commit run --files vllm/compilation/backends.py tests/compile/test_compiler_cache.py` — all applicable hooks passed.

## Details

The old `open(..., "w")` truncated the shared index before writing its replacement. The new file is created beside the target so `os.replace` is atomic on the same filesystem. The regression test checks that the previous index remains readable up to the replace and that no temporary file is left behind.

A search for open PRs containing `60410` found none immediately before opening.
